# PromptForge Expansion Package

Generated: 2026-08-29

This package adds twelve Builder categories and 720 structured Library work orders. Each category contains 60 records: the first 10 are FREE and the remaining 50 are LOCKED. The records use IDs 3411–4130 and follow the existing ROLE / CONTEXT / TASK / REQUIREMENTS / OUTPUT convention.

## A. New Categories Overview

| Category | Positioning | Work orders | Access split |
|---|---|---:|---|
| Email Marketing & Sequences | Campaigns, lifecycle & inbox conversion | 60 | 10 FREE / 50 LOCKED |
| SEO & Content Optimization | Search intent, briefs & measurable discoverability | 60 | 10 FREE / 50 LOCKED |
| Finance, Accounting & Admin | Clear records, controls & operating routines | 60 | 10 FREE / 50 LOCKED |
| Education & Learning | Lessons, practice & learner progress | 60 | 10 FREE / 50 LOCKED |
| Personal Development & Productivity | Focus, habits & reflective systems | 60 | 10 FREE / 50 LOCKED |
| HR / Recruitment & People Ops | Hiring, onboarding & healthy team systems | 60 | 10 FREE / 50 LOCKED |
| Ecommerce & Product | Offers, catalogues & product growth | 60 | 10 FREE / 50 LOCKED |
| WhatsApp / Messaging Business | Permission-first conversations that move business forward | 60 | 10 FREE / 50 LOCKED |
| Legal / Contracts & Compliance | Plain-language review support with professional boundaries | 60 | 10 FREE / 50 LOCKED |
| Real Estate | Listings, leads & property operations | 60 | 10 FREE / 50 LOCKED |
| Agency / Client Management | Scoping, communication & delivery confidence | 60 | 10 FREE / 50 LOCKED |
| Healthcare / Wellness | Clear, safe education and service communication | 60 | 10 FREE / 50 LOCKED |

## B. Category materials and work orders

## Email Marketing & Sequences

**Builder field schema:** `Campaign or lifecycle stage`, `Offer or message`, `Audience segment`, `Email sequence length`, `Primary CTA`, `Tone`, `Compliance and unsubscribe constraints`, `Reference examples`.

**Sample live-forged prompt:**

ROLE: You are an lifecycle email strategist and conversion copy editor.

CONTEXT: I need help with a welcome sequence for [organisation_or_project] serving [audience] in [location_or_market]. The current situation is [current_state]. The desired action or outcome is [desired_outcome]. Use [channel_or_format], [tone], and [timeframe].

TASK: map the context, audience, inputs, constraints, and desired outcome for this welcome sequence. Treat the category inputs as the source of truth and do not fill missing facts with guesses.

REQUIREMENTS:
- Use only supplied facts; mark assumptions clearly; never fabricate testimonials, customer results, credentials, prices, availability, or guarantees; keep personally identifiable information to the minimum needed; and make every variable easy to replace.
- Keep claims supportable, respect privacy, and make the next action clear for the intended audience.
- Prefer concise, mobile-readable language and label every replacement field in [square brackets].
- Explain the reasoning behind important choices without padding the answer.

OUTPUT:
1. State the objective, audience, assumptions, and success signal.
2. Produce the recommended welcome sequence in a clearly labelled structure.
3. Include a practical checklist, example, or reusable template where it improves execution.
4. Identify risks, approval points, and the next action for the responsible owner.
5. Add one focused improvement experiment and the evidence needed to review it.

WORK ORDER: 3411 / Discovery & planning.

## Legal / Contracts & Compliance

**Builder field schema:** `Document type`, `Jurisdiction`, `Parties and roles`, `Business objective`, `Key obligations`, `Risk tolerance`, `Review deadline`, `Counsel escalation points`.

**Sample live-forged prompt:**

ROLE: You are a legal-operations assistant focused on plain-language document workflows.

CONTEXT: I need help with a contract issue-spotting checklist for [organisation_or_project] serving [audience] in [location_or_market]. The current situation is [current_state]. The desired action or outcome is [desired_outcome]. Use [channel_or_format], [tone], and [timeframe].

TASK: map the context, audience, inputs, constraints, and desired outcome for this contract issue-spotting checklist. Treat the category inputs as the source of truth and do not fill missing facts with guesses.

REQUIREMENTS:
- Use only supplied facts; mark assumptions clearly; never fabricate testimonials, customer results, credentials, prices, availability, or guarantees; keep personally identifiable information to the minimum needed; and make every variable easy to replace.
- This is drafting and issue-spotting support, not legal advice. State jurisdictional assumptions, avoid presenting conclusions as legal opinions, and route material risks to a qualified lawyer.
- Prefer concise, mobile-readable language and label every replacement field in [square brackets].
- Explain the reasoning behind important choices without padding the answer.

OUTPUT:
1. State the objective, audience, assumptions, and success signal.
2. Produce the recommended contract issue-spotting checklist in a clearly labelled structure.
3. Include a practical checklist, example, or reusable template where it improves execution.
4. Identify risks, approval points, and the next action for the responsible owner.
5. Add one focused improvement experiment and the evidence needed to review it.

---

## Real Estate

**Builder field schema:** `Property type and location`, `Availability facts`, `Ideal prospect`, `Price or budget`, `Viewing or enquiry action`, `Channel`, `Verification and disclosure constraints`, `Follow-up cadence`.

**Sample live-forged prompt:**

## C. Implementation notes for engineering

The Builder should export these category definitions from a shared module instead of duplicating labels in the page component. On category change, reset the dependent topic and platform fields to the first valid option for the selected category, preserve the user’s audience and constraints when safe, and show the relevant safety note for Legal, Healthcare, Finance, and WhatsApp workflows. Every field should have an explicit label, keyboard access, a mobile-friendly control, and a replaceable variable value.

The Library importer should insert the JSON file into the existing `prompts` table with `ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE`, preserving the existing ID convention and exact `FREE` / `LOCKED` values. The public catalog should continue returning locked metadata with an empty prompt body until the viewer has lifetime access or an admin role. Search must cover title, category, and tags; category filters must use the exact labels in this package.

The quality score can be computed from weighted checks: role specificity, concrete task, audience, channel, goal, constraints, deliverables, examples, and safety boundaries. Expose the score with a short explanation and one-click suggestions that append or revise only the missing dimension. Variables should use `{{variable_name}}` in saved prompts while preserving the existing square-bracket replacement convention in catalogue templates.

Collections should be protected by the authenticated user ID. Add collections, collection memberships, prompt tags, and saved-prompt versions as separate tables or normalized relations; never accept a user ID from the client. Every mutation must derive ownership from `ctx.user.id`, and version snapshots should be immutable once written.

Multi-platform optimization should be an explicit control with targets for ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini, Grok, Midjourney, and Flux. Text assistants should receive structured instruction, context, constraints, and output format; image tools should receive subject, composition, lighting, aspect ratio, negative prompt, and reference guidance. Refine controls should be typed actions such as shorter, more formal, more creative, add examples, simplify, and strengthen constraints.

Exports should support Markdown, plain text, JSON, and a Notion-ready Markdown variant with headings and checklists. Keep exports local unless the user explicitly saves a copy. Mobile/PWA work should prioritize normal document scrolling, large touch targets, safe-area padding, offline shell caching, install metadata, reduced-motion support, and no sticky controls that cover Library cards.

## D. Suggested priority order

1. Ship the twelve categories, shared Builder schemas, and the 720-record catalog import because these immediately expand discoverability and user value.
2. Add variable insertion, quality scoring, and the improved Refine controls because they strengthen the core Forge loop without requiring a large data-model change.
3. Add collections, favorites, tags, and immutable version history with ownership tests.
4. Add multi-platform optimization and Notion-ready exports, then instrument usage to see which targets matter most.
5. Finish PWA installability, offline shell behavior, mobile QA, and accessibility regression checks.

## Safety boundaries

- **Legal / Contracts & Compliance:** This is drafting and issue-spotting support, not legal advice. State jurisdictional assumptions, avoid presenting conclusions as legal opinions, and route material risks to a qualified lawyer.
- **Healthcare / Wellness:** Keep content educational and non-diagnostic. Do not invent clinical facts, patient outcomes, dosages, or credentials; include urgent-care escalation when appropriate and defer to qualified clinicians.
- **Finance, Accounting & Admin:** Do not invent balances, transactions, tax conclusions, or regulatory obligations. Label estimates, preserve confidentiality, and route regulated decisions to a qualified professional.
- **WhatsApp / Messaging Business:** Use permission-based messaging only. Do not recommend scraping, unsolicited bulk outreach, or collection of unnecessary personal data; include opt-out, HELP, and human hand-off paths.
